Ablating each of the four components (the VSA, the Independent Controller, MCP tool access, and reflection-guided recovery) supports their separable contributions. Removing either core-state component drops YouRA below the full system. Code: https://github.com/PrayPrey/Your-Research-Agent.

</details>


### [70] [Beyond State-of-the-Art: Standardising Environmental Impact Metrics for AI Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01116)
*Lachlan McGinness,Dan Pagendam,Robert Offner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对LLM环境足迹增加但缺乏碳核算标准的问题，本文通过分析NeurIPS 2025论文发现环境影响报告几乎为零。为此提出标准化可持续性指标和推理碳成本估算启发式方法，开发了carbonbenchmark软件工具，并正式提出“最小完成任务模型（SMAJ）”框架，旨在推动领域从追求边际精度转向优先计算效率和环境责任。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLMs）的能力增强和普及带来了巨大的环境足迹，尽管有呼吁负责任AI，但机器学习社区缺乏标准化的碳核算实践，且现有研究极少报道环境影响。

Method: 1. 对NeurIPS 2025接受的5,285篇论文进行自动化文献综述；2. 定义评估模型训练效率的标准化可持续性指标及LLM推理碳成本的简单估算启发式规则；3. 开发carbonbenchmark这一即插即用软件解决方案用于追踪和报告排放；4. 形式化提出“最小完成任务模型（SMAJ）”框架。

Result: 揭示了当前学术报告中环境impact的缺失现状；提供了具体的衡量标准和软件工具；确立了将计算效率与环境责任置于与传统SotA精度同等重要地位的新范式。

Conclusion: 为了对抗以不成比例的环境代价换取微小精度提升的趋势，应正式采纳SMAJ框架，鼓励整个领域优先考虑计算效率、环境问责制，而不仅仅是传统的最先进（SotA）精度。

Abstract: As the capabilities and ubiquity of Large Language Models (LLMs) grow, so does their environmental footprint. Despite calls for responsible AI, the machine learning community lacks standardised practices for carbon accounting. Our automated literature review of the 5,285 papers accepted to NeurIPS 2025 reveals that reporting of environmental impact is nearly non-existent. To catalyse a shift toward sustainable AI, we define standardised sustainability metrics for evaluating model training efficiency, accompanied by simple heuristics to estimate the carbon cost of LLM inference. We implement these metrics in carbonbenchmark, a drop-in software solution for tracking and reporting emissions.
  Finally, to combat the pursuit of marginal accuracy gains at disproportionate environmental costs, we formalise the `Smallest Model that Achieves the Job' (SMAJ), a framework which challenges the field to prioritise computational efficiency and environmental accountability alongside traditional `State-of-the-Art' (SotA) accuracy.

</details>


### [71] [AbsorbEvo: An Agentic Framework for Autonomous Inverse Design of Microwave Absorbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01119)
*Zhicheng Feng,Yubo Zhao,Xuefeng Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AbsorbEvo，一个基于大语言模型的自主逆向设计智能体框架，将自然语言性能目标转化为微波吸波材料设计，并通过结合语言推理、物理预测和全波仿真验证，显著提升了设计成功率和覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统高性能微波吸波器的设计依赖电磁理论、材料科学和仿真编程的专业知识，且优化过程耗时繁琐；需要一种能自动化、降低门槛并提高效率的设计方法。

Method: 1. 构建AbsorbEvo智能体框架：利用大型语言模型（LLM）根据任务目标和计算历史提出参数调整方向及幅度；2. 混合候选生成策略：结合定向增量与全局采样生成候选方案；3. 低效过滤机制：使用低成本预测模型作为物理先验对候选者进行初步排序筛选；4. 精确验证流程：仅对高排名设计进行耗时的全波仿真，通过物理有效性检查后用于评估性能并指导后续搜索；5. 技能蒸馏：从训练任务中提取文本化经验技能，并在独立验证后应用于新任务。

Result: 在AbsorbBench-36基准测试中，在相同的提案预算下：AbsorbEvo的任务成功率为79.17%，优于通用智能体的25.00%和随机搜索的12.50%；其平均最佳覆盖率（mean best coverage）为0.7816，显著高于通用智能体的0.6434和随机搜索的0.6448。

Conclusion: AbsorbEvo通过将语言推理与基于物理的反馈整合到设计决策中，为自然语言驱动的微波吸波器自主逆向设计提供了方法论基础，有效解决了传统设计中专业知识要求高和优化效率低的问题。

Abstract: Designing high-performance microwave absorbers requires specialized expertise in electromagnetic theory, materials science and simulation programming, and entails time-consuming optimization. Here, we present AbsorbEvo, an agentic framework for autonomous inverse design that translates natural-language performance objectives into designs verified by full-wave simulations. Its candidate evolution strategy integrates language reasoning, physics-based prediction and historical feedback. A large language model proposes the directions and magnitudes of parameter adjustments based on task objectives and computational history. The system combines directed increments with global sampling to generate candidates and uses a low-cost predictive model as a physics prior to rank them. Only high-ranking designs undergo full-wave simulation. Results passing physical validity checks are used to evaluate performance and guide subsequent search. Experience from training tasks is further distilled into textual skills, which are independently validated before use in new tasks. Under identical proposal budgets on held-out AbsorbBench-36 tasks, AbsorbEvo achieved a task success rate of 79.17%, versus 25.00% for a generic agent and 12.50% for random search. Its mean best coverage was 0.7816, compared with 0.6434 and 0.6448, respectively. By integrating language reasoning and physics-based feedback into design decisions, AbsorbEvo provides a methodological foundation for natural-language-driven autonomous inverse design of microwave absorbers.

</details>


### [72] [CortexBridge: Cortical Alignment of EEG Montages for Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01124)
*Jiazhen Hong,Xiaotian Zhou,Zihao Ding,Kailong Wang,Yu Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CortexBridge，通过结合电极和图谱坐标将任意脑电图蒙太奇映射到共享皮层潜在空间，解决EEG基础模型在不同电极布局间迁移困难的问题，显著提升了跨数据集分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的脑电图（EEG）基础模型通常使用固定的通道词汇表或有限的蒙太吉集进行预训练，当电极布局发生变化时，导致迁移学习困难。

Method: 提出了一个轻量级适配器CortexBridge，该适配器结合EEG特征与电极及图谱坐标，将任意蒙太奇映射到一个共享的皮层潜在空间。在三个冻结的基础模型上，基于MOABB的五种BCI数据集进行评估。

Result: 在15项评估中的13项提高了性能。平衡准确率平均提升幅度分别为：EEGPT 0.80%，LaBraM 0.70%，CBraMod 3.26%。在12类稳态视觉诱发电位（SSVEP）分类任务中最大增益达13.02%。可视化显示，SSVEP的表示集中在Yeo视觉网络，而听觉P300则不同。

Conclusion: 建立了皮层对齐作为一种可学习的、基于解剖学的路由机制，用于将异构EEG蒙太奇连接到预训练基础模型，证明了其在处理不同电极布局时的有效性和通用性。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) foundation models are often pretrained with a fixed channel vocabulary or a limited set of montages, making transfer difficult when electrode layouts change. We propose CortexBridge, a lightweight adapter that combines EEG features with electrode and atlas coordinates to map arbitrary montages into a shared cortical latent space. Evaluated with three frozen foundation models on five brain-computer interface (BCI) datasets from the Mother of All BCI Benchmarks (MOABB), CortexBridge improves performance in 13 of 15 evaluations. The gains in balanced accuracy average 0.80% for EEGPT, 0.70% for LaBraM, and 3.26% for CBraMod, with a maximum gain of 13.02% on 12-class steady-state visual evoked potential (SSVEP) classification. Visualizations of the learned atlas representations reveal task-dependent spatial patterns, with SSVEP showing a more concentrated representation in the Yeo Visual network than auditory P300. These results establish cortical alignment as a learnable and anatomically grounded routing mechanism from heterogeneous EEG montages to pretrained foundation models.

</details>


### [73] [Grounding Large Language Models in DSGE Simulators for Policy Generation and Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01128)
*Aditya Dubey,Namah Gupta,Vinti Agarwal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文通过将指令微调语言模型嵌入六个动态随机一般均衡（DSGE）模拟器，测试其生成的经济政策响应是否符合实际经济动态。研究发现，由于长周期信用分配问题，缺乏学习价值函数的GRPO无法区分导致最终结果的具体早期动作，因此主要使用具备广义优势估计功能的PPO方法，旨在通过模拟经济后果而非仅凭语言合理性来评估政策效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型能生成看似合理的经济政策响应，但这并未证明其行动符合经济动态规律。需要从语言合理性转向基于实际模拟经济后果的政策评估。

Method: 将指令微调语言模型置于六个Snowdrop支持的DSGE模拟器中，模型根据经济和话语变化选择有界政策行动并获得奖励状态。实现Python接口进行重复回放、持久冲击等。以PPO为主要方法（利用价值函数和广义优势估计传播延迟奖励），以无价值函数的GRPO为基准（因无法区分早期动作责任而存在局限）。实验还测试了方向性语义信号、奖励 horizon、轨迹热身启动、跨模拟器迁移及历史锚定冲击。

Result: 揭示了在长周期信用分配场景下，GRPO因无学习价值函数且依赖完整回放的组内相对优势，导致若所有回放回报相同则归一化优势为零，无法追溯具体因果动作；相比之下，PPO能有效处理延迟奖励传播。

Conclusion: 应依据模拟的经济后果而非单纯的语言合理性来评判政策行动，证明了在宏观经济模拟环境中，PPO比GRPO更适合解决此类长周期信用分配问题。

Abstract: Large language models can produce economic policy responses that sound reasonable, but this does not show that their actions are consistent with economic dynamics. We test this by placing an instruction-tuned language model inside six Snowdrop-backed dynamic stochastic general equilibrium (DSGE) simulators. At each turn, the model observes the economy and a change in economic discourse, selects a bounded policy action, and receives the next simulated state and an economic reward. We implement a common Python interface for repeated rollouts, persistent shocks, state cloning, and rolling-horizon simulation.
  This setting creates a long-horizon credit-assignment problem. Policy effects may appear several quarters after an action is taken. PPO has a learned value function that can propagate delayed reward to earlier tokens through generalized advantage estimation. GRPO has no learned value function and instead assigns a group-relative advantage from complete rollout returns. It therefore cannot distinguish which earlier turn caused the outcome; if every rollout receives the same return, the normalized advantage is zero. We use PPO as the primary method and GRPO as a matched critic-free baseline. The experiments also test directional semantic signals, reward horizon, trajectory warm starts, cross-simulator transfer, and historically anchored pandemic and monetary-policy shocks. The objective is to judge policy actions by their simulated economic consequences rather than by plausible language alone.

</details>


### [74] [Auditing Action Settlement in LLM Agent Environments: Order, Progress, and Replay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01138)
*Haotian Chen,Bowen Ye,Yuning Zhang,Jingkun Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究大语言模型（LLM）智能体环境中并发行动的仲裁机制，通过测试五种结算策略发现“随机票选”比“保守拒绝”显著提升了多智能体任务完成率（90.28% vs 31.25%），并验证了系统在执行语义层面的回放一致性与抗干扰能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在LLM智能体环境中，即使单个行动提议有效，并发执行仍需仲裁机制来解决冲突。现有方法可能在处理顺序敏感性和独立小实例最优解时存在不足，需评估不同策略的公正性、效率及一致性。

Method: 实现了一种类型化快照-结算合约（typed snapshot-settlement contract）。进行了28,800次穷举排列试验和2,160次脚本化多步片段实验，测试五种不同的结算策略。重点分析了顺序敏感性、有用进展和回放一致性三个属性。此外，通过完全状态日志审计验证了回放准确性和腐败检测能力。

Result: 联合策略在固定优先级下具有空间顺序不变性。在六智能体门道任务中，“保守拒绝”策略仅完成31.25%的智能体，而“随机票选”策略完成率达90.28%，提升幅度为59.03个百分点（95%置信区间：50.00-68.06）。所有策略均保持了测试的空间约束，但优先级仲裁未能达到独立小实例的最优解。审计显示系统能精确重放156个检查点，并拒绝1,332种构造的损坏数据。

Conclusion: 仲裁策略的选择对多智能体系统的执行效率和可用性有决定性影响。虽然优先级仲裁是常见方案，但其性能劣于简单的随机分配机制。研究强调了执行语义的重要性，表明良好的仲裁机制应兼顾顺序不变性与高完成率，且不依赖人类现实性或长期公平性假设。

Abstract: Concurrent actions in large language model (LLM) agent environments require arbitration even when each proposal is individually valid. We implement a typed snapshot-settlement contract and audit three distinct properties: order sensitivity, useful progress, and replay consistency. Five settlement policies are tested in 28,800 exhaustive permutation trials and 2,160 scripted multistep episodes. Joint policies are spatially order-invariant conditional on fixed priorities, yet conservative rejection completes only 31.25% of agents in a six-agent doorway task versus 90.28% for random tickets; the paired improvement is 59.03 percentage points (95% bootstrap interval: 50.00-68.06). All policies preserve the tested spatial constraints, and priority arbitration still misses the independent small-instance optimum. A separate full-state journal audit exactly replays 156 checkpoints and rejects 1,332 constructed corruptions with a retained terminal anchor. The evidence concerns execution semantics, not human realism or long-run fairness.

</details>


### [75] [ReSolve: Reusing Candidate Reasoning through Selective Generative Moderation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01140)
*Bangji Yang,Jiajun Fan,Hongba Ma,Xi Zhu,Weizhi Zhang,Minghao Guo,Ye Li,Hamid Palangi,Jiaxuan You*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ReSolve，一种无需训练的推理方法，通过选择性生成式验证重用中间推导过程，在保证高准确率的同时显著减少Token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决传统采样多个解决方案时计算资源浪费在中间推导和未完成的论证上的问题，旨在更高效地利用候选推理过程以获取最终答案。

Method: 引入ReSolve，这是一种基于选择性生成式调节的推断程序。通过答案分布控制器在候选答案不一致或缺可解析答案时调用模型检查现有推导，并将生成的解纳入有界循环中。使用混合评分法评估130道竞赛数学问题，与投票法、自一致性等方法进行对比。

Result: 在两独立候选池测试中，ReSolve分别获得100和99个正确答案，优于4选1投票法（91和92）及八样本自一致性（94和96）。ReSolve节省约46%-47%的Token消耗。消融实验显示，若移除可见推导仅保留答案键，准确率降至93且计算增加；选择性调节相比始终开启调节可减少54%的调节Token和6.2%的总Pipeline Token。

Conclusion: 证明了候选推理作为可重用的推断计算的潜力，该方法能在不牺牲准确性的前提下提高效率，但未证明其相对于额外采样或专门路线指令具有显著的精度优势。

Abstract: Sampling multiple solutions spends computation on intermediate deductions and unfinished arguments as well as final answers. We introduce ReSolve, a training-free inference procedure that reuses this candidate reasoning through selective generative moderation. An answer-distribution controller invokes a model to examine existing derivations when candidates disagree or lack a parseable answer, then incorporates the generated solution into a bounded loop. Under Hybrid scoring on 130 competition-mathematics problems evaluated with two independently sampled candidate pools, ReSolve obtains 100 and 99 correct answers, compared with 91 and 92 for voting over the same four candidates, with no correct-to-incorrect changes relative to that vote in either pool. Eight-sample self-consistency obtains 94 and 96 correct answers while consuming substantially more tokens; ReSolve uses 46.3% and 47.2% fewer tokens in the two evaluations. A controlled ablation removes visible derivations while retaining answer keys, vote counts, and the per-state output-cap rule, reducing accuracy from 100 to 93 correct despite increasing computation. Selective and always-on Uniform moderation both solve 97 problems, while selectivity reduces moderation tokens by approximately 54% and total pipeline tokens by 6.2%. These results support candidate reasoning as reusable inference computation. They do not establish an accuracy advantage over additional sampling or a distinct benefit from specialized route instructions.

</details>


### [76] [When Does Exercise-Specific Joint Selection Help? An Audit of Evaluation and Control Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01188)
*Haotian Chen,Jingkun Yu,Yuning Zhang,Bowen Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过审计REHAB24-6数据集，发现运动特定的关节选择在提升骨架动作分类准确率上的增益微弱且结构敏感；研究未确立新的算法或临床益处，而是强调该领域 claims 需具备明确的评估指标及结构适当的对照。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在厘清“运动特定关节选择（Exercise-specific joint selection）”在提升骨架动作正确性分类方面的实际贡献与局限性，验证现有方法声称的增益是否具有统计学意义和结构性稳健性。

Method: 对10名受试者的1,057次重复动作数据进行审计，分析评估聚合、子集结构和时序表示的影响。使用kNN、RBF-SVM、逻辑回归及随机卷积比较器进行模型测试，并与1,000个维度匹配的随机映射结果进行对比，同时引入序列顺序和配对种子控制变量。

Result: 手动子集的kNN增益从合并分层AUROC的0.055降至人均等权重AUROC的0.020，两者置信区间均包含零。在随机对照中，仅14个随机映射达到或超过手动合并结果（当匹配双侧结构和躯干 inclusion 时增至145）。RBF-SVM保留正向人均增益，而逻辑回归和随机卷积比较器在该指标下呈负增益。

Conclusion: 这是一项探索性审计，表明关节选择的声明需要明确的评估标量（explicit estimands）和结构适当的对照才能成立；该方法并未建立新算法或证明明确的临床益处。

Abstract: Exercise-specific joint selection can improve skeleton-based correctness classification, but what does that gain establish? We audit 1,057 repetitions from ten REHAB24-6 subjects, separating evaluation aggregation, subset structure, and temporal representation. The manual-subset kNN gain changes from 0.055 for pooled out-of-fold AUROC to 0.020 for equal-weight within-person AUROC; both paired intervals include zero. Among 1,000 dimension-matched random maps, 14 match or exceed the manual pooled result, versus 145 when bilateral structure and trunk inclusion are also matched. RBF-SVM retains a positive within-person gain, whereas logistic regression and a random-convolution comparator have negative point gains under that estimand. Sequence-order and paired-seed controls further qualify the interpretation. This exploratory audit shows why joint-selection claims require explicit estimands and structurally appropriate controls; it does not establish a new algorithm or clinical benefit.

</details>


### [77] [Federated Agent Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01195)
*Qiang Yang,Zhiqiang Kou,Xueyi Zhang,Dong-Dong Wu,Hanlin Gu,Jing Guo,Yang Liu,Di Jiang,Qian Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出联邦智能体优化（FAO）框架，解决大型语言模型（LLM）智能体在隐私保护下无法共享经验导致能力受限的问题。通过抽象和聚合非参数化经验（如记忆、工具、技能），实现分布式智能体的协作增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在私有环境中积累的经验因隐私和商业限制而无法直接共享。传统联邦学习仅关注模型参数，不足以覆盖智能体包含记忆、工具使用、奖励机制和技能等更广泛的能力维度。

Method: 将联邦智能体优化（FAO）形式化为一个多目标优化问题，旨在平衡智能体效用、隐私泄露风险和通信成本。该方法在策略、记忆、工具使用、奖励及结构化知识/技能等多个层面组织优化空间，研究如何对私有经验进行抽象、保护、聚合并转化为可迁移的能力。

Result: 构建了一个统一视角，阐明了智能体如何在不直接共享原始数据和轨迹的前提下，通过受控的信息交换从其他智能体获益。定义了FAO的优化空间及其关键要素。

Conclusion: 确立了FAO作为提升分布式智能体协作能力的新范式，识别了该领域面临的关键挑战，并为构建值得信赖的联邦智能体系统指明了未来研究方向。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly operate in private environments and accumulate valuable experience from task execution, tool use, feedback, and local knowledge. Yet such experience is distributed across organizations and cannot be directly shared because of privacy and proprietary constraints. Conventional federated learning is insufficient for this setting, as agent capabilities extend beyond model parameters to memory, tools, rewards, skills, and structured knowledge. In this paper, we formulate \textbf{Federated Agent Optimization (FAO)}, which studies how distributed agents can collaboratively improve through controlled information exchange while keeping raw data, complete trajectories, and private knowledge local. We define FAO as a multi-objective problem balancing agent utility, privacy leakage, and communication cost, and organize its optimization space across policy, memory, tool use, reward, and structured knowledge and skills. We further characterize how private experience can be abstracted, protected, aggregated, and adapted into transferable capabilities, providing a unified view of how agents can benefit from one another without direct experience sharing. Finally, we identify the key challenges of FAO and outline several promising directions for future research toward trustworthy federated agent systems.

</details>


### [78] [Dependency-Aware Reward Shaping for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01207)
*Ziyi Chen,Yan Zhang,Jianhui Wei,Daoan Zhang,Zuozhu Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出依赖感知奖励塑形（DARS），通过构建任务进度的依赖图来解决强化学习中步骤信用分配不均的问题，显著提升了大语言模型在推理和智能体任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法在处理大型语言模型训练时，终端奖励对具体步骤的指导性不足。传统方法忽视了对未纠正错误所进行的后续工作无效、而独立工作仍有效这一事实，导致失败回合中即使有进展的步骤也因最终奖励为零而缺乏正向反馈。

Method: 提出DARS方法：将任务进度表示为由前置条件关系链接的谓词，并依据依赖图分配步骤级信用。注释者标记每步验证、失效或修复的谓词；已验证谓词根据距最近失效前置条件的图距离进行折扣，独立谓词不受影响。修复操作基于剩余错误更新权重，失效谓词需重新验证才能恢复信用。固定势函数将注释转化为有符号的每步奖励，并提供通用接口集成至GiGPO、ARPO/AEPO等方法。

Result: 在5个任务家族和1.5B至8B参数量的模型上进行评估：相比使用相同预算和ALFWorld环境的GiGPO，成功率高出最多10分；提升WebShop任务得分及Search-R1 QA准确率；与AEPO熵驱动训练结合提升AIME24/25表现；在1.7B和4B规模下超越OmniOPD。消融实验证明步骤级信用、依赖衰减和图拓扑均贡献显著。在ALFWorld上，蒸馏的8B注释者可替代API注释者实现高效运行。

Conclusion: DARS通过细粒度的依赖关系建模解决了强化学习中的信用分配问题，增强了模型在复杂推理和智能体任务中的性能，且具备良好的可扩展性和与其他训练方法的兼容性。

Abstract: When training large language models with reinforcement learning, terminal rewards provide little guidance about which steps matter. Common methods for assigning step credit overlook that work built on uncorrected mistakes is wasted while independent work remains valid. With only a final success/failure reward, every step in a failed episode has zero total future reward, even when it made progress. We propose Dependency-Aware Reward Shaping (DARS), which represents task progress as predicates linked by prerequisite relations and assigns step-level credit over the dependency graph. An annotator marks which predicates each step verifies, invalidates, or repairs. Verified predicates are discounted according to graph distance from the nearest broken prerequisite, while independent predicates are unaffected. Repairs update these weights based on any errors that remain; invalidated predicates need re-verification to regain credit. A fixed potential converts these annotations into signed per-step rewards. A common reward and annotation interface allows DARS to integrate with a range of reasoning and agentic training methods, such as GiGPO and ARPO/AEPO, without changing their rollout strategies or optimizers. Across five task families and models from 1.5B to 8B, DARS improves success by up to 10 points over GiGPO trained with the same budget and harness (ALFWorld), raises the WebShop task score and Search-R1 QA accuracy, complements AEPO's entropy-based training on AIME24/25 with a Python interpreter, and exceeds OmniOPD in controlled tool-free reasoning comparisons at 1.7B and 4B. Ablations show that step-level credit, dependency attenuation, and graph topology each contribute. On ALFWorld, a distilled 8B annotator matches the API annotator, enabling DARS to run efficiently without a frontier judge. Code is available at https://github.com/JianhuiWei7/DARS.

</details>


### [79] [Reputation, Strategy, and Emotion Effects on Generative AI Cooperation: A Comparison Across Reasoning and Non-Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01222)
*Celso de Melo,Zishan Feng,James Hale,Kazunori Terada,Giorgio Coricelli,Jonathan Gratch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究通过囚徒困境实验评估生成式AI（Gen AI）的合作行为，发现非推理模型受声誉、策略和情感信号共同影响；而具备推理能力的模型更侧重策略与声誉，情感作用减弱且呈现层级加权特征。不同模型表现出异质性的博弈行为，凸显了开发标准化合作基准以指导负责任部署的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成式AI在经济和社会关键互动中承担日益自主的角色，理解其合作倾向及塑造该倾向的信号已成为必要。旨在揭示前沿Gen AI模型在社会互动中的行为模式及其差异。

Method: 使用迭代 prisoner's dilemma（囚徒困境游戏作为实验框架），操纵三个变量：对手声誉（正/未知/负）、策略（敲诈 vs. 慷慨）和非语言情感信号（面部表情传达的竞争或合作评估）。对两组模型进行测试：1. 非推理模型（Claude 3.5, Gemini 2.0 Flash, GPT-4o）；2. 推理模型（Claude 4.6, Gemini 3, GPT-5.2）。

Result: 1. 非推理模型：合作行为受所有三个因素系统性地塑造，模仿人类行为模式，但各模型权重分配差异显著。
2. 推理模型：更集中依赖策略和声誉；消除了非推理模型中观察到的“波将金效应”（在诊断性和谐游戏中表现为几乎普遍的合作）；情感作用更具条件性，符合层级线索加权策略而非简单的社会敏感度丧失。
3. 终局行为异质性：推理模型表现出从持续合作到系统性最后一轮背叛的不同结束行为，揭示了具有直接实践相关性的特定模型可剥削性谱系。

Conclusion: 生成式AI模型是日益复杂但异质的社会行动者。研究强调了开发标准化合作基准对于在交互式、对社会产生重大影响的环境中负责任地部署Gen AI具有实际价值。

Abstract: As generative AI (Gen AI) systems take on increasingly autonomous roles in economically and socially consequential interactions, understanding their propensity to cooperate -- and the signals that shape this propensity -- has become essential. We examine cooperative behavior in frontier Gen AI models using the iterated prisoner's dilemma, manipulating counterpart reputation (positive, unknown, negative), strategy (extortion vs. generosity), and non-verbal emotional signaling (facial expressions conveying competitive or cooperative appraisals). In a first study with non-reasoning models (Claude 3.5, Gemini 2.0 Flash, GPT-4o), cooperation was systematically shaped by all three factors, paralleling patterns long documented in human behavioral research, though models varied substantially in how heavily each factor was weighted. A second study with reasoning models (Claude 4.6, Gemini 3, GPT-5.2) revealed a more concentrated reliance on strategy and reputation, a near-elimination of the Potemkin effect observed in non-reasoning models (evidenced by near-uniform cooperation in a diagnostic harmony game), and a more conditional role for emotion consistent with a hierarchical cue-weighting strategy rather than a simple loss of social sensitivity. Reasoning models also showed heterogeneous end-game behavior, ranging from sustained cooperation to systematic last-round defection effect, revealing model-specific exploitability profiles with direct practical relevance for deployment in negotiation and other multi-round interactions. Together, these findings characterize Gen AI models as increasingly sophisticated, though heterogeneous, social actors, and underscore the practical value of developing standardized cooperation benchmarks to inform the responsible deployment of Gen AI in interactive, socially consequential settings.

</details>


### [80] [HHR: Hierarchical Hash Retrieval for Efficient LLM Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01230)
*Lianjun Liu,Tiantian Zheng,You Huang,Weiqi Yan,Mingte Qiu,Huazhong Liu,Xiaofeng Zhu,Yunshan Zhong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出层级哈希检索（HHR）框架，通过几何感知键路由（GKR）和学习哈希投影（LHP），解决哈希检索中因丢弃幅度信息导致的与注意力相关性不匹配问题，显著提升长上下文推理的效率和准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于哈希的检索方法将查询和键编码为二进制码并利用汉明距离进行选择，但汉明距离忽略了特征幅度信息，导致低逻辑值的键被误召回（假阳性）和高逻辑值的键被遗漏（假阴性），造成检索结果与注意力相关性严重失配。

Method: 提出层级哈希检索（HHR）框架：1. 几何感知键路由（GKR）：学习头正交变换以重新分布特征幅度，推导更具判别力的页面级逻辑值界限，用于粗粒度剪枝；2. 学习哈希投影（LHP）：学习头投影空间，使汉明距离与真实的查询-键相关性排名对齐，用于细粒度检索。

Result: 在多个LLM和基准测试中优于现有方法。例如在LongBench上平均得分提升1.10分；在128K上下文长度下，对Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct实现3.30x解码加速比和2.83x端到端加速比，同时大幅减少假阳性和假阴性。

Conclusion: HHR通过结合粗粒度和细粒度优化，有效抑制了哈希稀疏注意力中的检索错误，显著提升了长上下文大语言模型推理的计算效率和输出保真度。

Abstract: Efficient long-context inference is essential for large language models (LLMs), yet it poses a severe computational bottleneck. Hash-based retrieval offers an efficient alternative by encoding queries and keys into binary codes and using Hamming distance for key selection. However, this leads to a critical mismatch between Hamming distance and attention relevance. Query-Key logits depend jointly on directional similarity and feature magnitudes, whereas hash binarization discards magnitude information, causing both false-positive retrieval of low-logit keys and false-negative omission of high-logit keys. To address these failures, we propose Hierarchical Hash Retrieval (HHR), a coarse-to-fine framework that progressively improves retrieval accuracy through Geometry-Aware Key Routing (GKR) and Learned Hash Projection (LHP). GKR learns a head-wise orthogonal transformation to redistribute feature magnitudes and derive more discriminative page-level logit bounds, enabling effective pruning of low-logit keys while preserving important candidates. LHP then learns a head-wise projection space that aligns Hamming distance with the true Query-Key relevance ranking for fine-grained retrieval. By combining GKR and LHP, HHR suppresses false positives and recovers false negatives, substantially improving the fidelity of hash-based sparse attention. Extensive experiments across diverse LLMs and benchmarks demonstrate that HHR achieves superior performance over existing methods. For example, on LongBench, HHR improves the average score by 1.10 points and, at a context length of 128K, achieves up to a 3.30x decoding speedup and a 2.83x end-to-end speedup for Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/lianjunl13-sudo/HHR.

</details>


### [81] [Learning to Ask: Information Acquisition for SLM-LLM Collaboration, under a budget](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01236)
*Yongjun Kim,Xiaoxiao Li,Jaeho Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于RLVR框架的SLM-LLM协作模式，将协作建模为信息获取问题而非计算分配。SLM作为主推理器仅在必要时向黑盒LLM咨询，通过精准查询而非整体委托来提升效率。该方法在数学推理和代码任务中优化了性能-成本权衡，并具备跨模型迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SLM与LLM协作方法多侧重于计算分配（即决定哪部分由哪个模型处理），但在黑盒API场景下，这种粗粒度委托或上下文重复传输导致效率低下。旨在解决API预算约束下的低效问题，探索更灵活的信息交互机制。

Method: 1. 将协作重新定义为受API预算约束的信息获取问题；2. 开发三阶段强化学习视觉推理（RLVR）框架，联合优化顾问调用时机、查询构建策略及信息整合方式；3. SLM保持主推理地位，按需向黑盒LLM发出针对性查询。

Result: 在数学推理和编程任务上，该方法显著改善了性能-成本比，优于现有协作基线；在某些场景下达到或超越 oracle problem-level routing（最优级别的任务路由）的效果；且无需额外训练即可将策略迁移到其他顾问模型家族。

Conclusion: 面向黑盒API环境的细粒度、按需协作范式优于传统的计算分配范式。通过RLVR框架实现的“主从式”信息查询策略能有效平衡小模型效率与大模型推理能力，具有通用性和高迁移价值。

Abstract: Collaboration between a small language model (SLM) and a large language model (LLM) offers an opportunity to combine the efficiency of smaller models with the strong reasoning capabilities of larger ones. Existing approaches primarily frame such collaboration as a computation allocation problem, determining which model should handle each portion of the reasoning process. In black-box API-based settings, however, this paradigm can be inefficient due to coarse-grained delegation or repeated transmission of context across model switches. In this work, we instead formulate SLM-LLM collaboration as an information acquisition problem, under an API budget constraint. The SLM remains the primary reasoner and selectively queries a black-box LLM advisor only when needed, issuing targeted queries rather than delegating the reasoning process itself. To realize this strategy, we develop a three-stage RLVR framework that learns whether to call the advisor, how to formulate useful queries, and how to integrate the collaboration into the reasoning process by jointly refining advisor invocation and information use. Across mathematical reasoning and coding tasks, our approach improves the performance--cost tradeoff over existing collaboration baselines and, in some settings, matches or exceeds oracle problem-level routing. Finally, we show that our strategy can transfer to other advisor model families, without further training.

</details>


### [82] [Revision-Aware Independent Agent Graphs for Dynamic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01249)
*Yan Luo,Selim-Antoine Lali,Jeremy Moebel,Iliass Khoutaibi,Ahmadou Aidara,Mengyu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出动态任务路由场景，解决传统静态协议无法处理历史更新的问题。引入RIAG多智能体策略，通过缓存和解耦时序解析，实现高精度与低调用次数的平衡，显著优于基准方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统推理协议任务固定，无法测试智能体在动态环境中是否保留有效更新、维持未受影响的工作或重建历史任务绑定。需要研究能处理事件流修订任务绑定的动态场景。

Method: 将MMLU等6个基准重构为31,119个动态片段；提出Revision-Aware Independent Agent Graph (RIAG)，这是一种有界的多智能体策略，将确定性时序解析与任务推理分离。通过不可变文档身份缓存解决方案，使用审计和修复机制，限制每次文档版本调用次数。

Result: 同质RIAG以0.62次/查询的调用成本达到54.24%的路由与答案联合准确率；异构RIAG达到49.78%。对比最强基线方法（18.00次/查询调用，准确率32.22%），RIAG在效率和质量上均有显著提升。

Conclusion: RIAG有效解决了动态任务路由中的权衡问题（重新计算vs过时结论），证明了在有限调用下通过解耦时序处理和多智能体协作可实现高效的动态推理。

Abstract: Conventional reasoning protocols present a fixed, preselected task, so they cannot test whether an agent propagates relevant updates, preserves unaffected work, or reconstructs a historical task binding. We therefore study \emph{dynamic task routing}, in which an event stream revises task bindings and a system must select the document version valid at each query time before solving it. To study this problem, we repurpose six widely used benchmarks: MMLU, MMLU-Pro, MedMCQA, MATH, GPQA, and HumanEval into 31{,}119 dynamic episodes comprising 373{,}428 temporally categorized queries. This setting exposes a central trade-off: recomputing after every event wastes work, whereas unguarded reuse returns stale conclusions. We introduce the Revision-Aware Independent Agent Graph (RIAG), a bounded multi-agent policy that separates deterministic temporal resolution from task reasoning. RIAG caches solutions by immutable document identity, starts each fresh task with two unexposed attempts, and conditionally invokes audit and repair, using at most four calls per document version. On this collection, homogeneous RIAG achieves 54.24\% joint routing-and-answer accuracy at 0.62 calls/query, compared with 32.22\% at 18.00 calls/query for the strongest comparison method; heterogeneous RIAG reaches 49.78\% at 0.63 calls/query.

</details>


### [83] [DeFA: Dependency-Guided Failure Attribution for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01256)
*Bo Deng,Xinlei Zheng,Yi Wei,Kang Zhou,Chongyang Tao,Renzhao Liang,Xuanren Chen,Lifan Guo,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了DeFA框架，一种基于依赖关系的智能体失败归因系统。通过构建事件依赖图和失败传播图，DeFA能在长轨迹中精准定位决定性错误、责任智能体及错误类别。该方法在处理多模态（图像/视频）轨迹方面同样有效，其诊断反馈能显著提升下游任务的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLM）智能体执行过程中的错误及其可见后果往往相隔多个步骤，导致决定性的错误难以定位。现有的方法难以同时理解步骤内容和步骤间的依赖关系，因此需要一种能够有效分析长轨迹中因果关系和错误的机制。

Method: DeFA框架包含以下核心步骤：
1. 构建**事件依赖图**：结合协议关系和语义依赖，覆盖整个执行轨迹。
2. 构建**失败传播图**：识别可能违反任务要求的事件，追踪其来源和后续影响以构建故障传播路径。
3. **局部-全局上下文结合**：为应对长轨迹，将执行分割为片段，结合当前片段的详细内容与其余片段的摘要，实现具有全局上下文的局部诊断。
4. **错误判定**利用步骤证据和其在故障传播中的作用，确定决定性错误、责任智能体和错误类别。

Result: 1. 在"Who and When"及其Pro文本子集上，DeFA在所有评估的基础模型上实现了最高的责任智能体准确率和确切步骤准确率，并在Pro数据集上实现了同类别对齐方法中最高的故障模式准确率。
2. 在图像和视频轨迹上的实验证明了其在多模态失败归因中的适用性。
3. 消融实验验证了分段策略、事件依赖图和失败传播图的贡献。
4. 使用DeFA的诊断反馈进行技能演化（Trace2Skill），使下游任务准确率比原生流程提高了6-15个百分点。

Conclusion: DeFA提供了一种有效的依赖引导的失败归因方法，不仅解决了长轨迹中错误定位难的问题，还通过提供高质量的诊断反馈促进了智能体技能的演化和后续任务性能的提升，展示了其在调试和改进LLM智能体方面的实用价值。

Abstract: Errors in LLM agent executions and their visible consequences can be separated by many steps, making decisive-error localization a matter of understanding both step content and step dependencies. We introduce DeFA, a dependency-guided framework for agent failure attribution. DeFA first combines protocol relations and semantic dependencies into an event dependency graph spanning the trajectory. It then identifies events that may violate task requirements and traces their sources and subsequent effects to construct a failure propagation graph. Finally, DeFA uses step evidence and the steps' roles in failure propagation to identify the decisive error, responsible agent, and error category. To support long trajectories, DeFA partitions executions into segments and combines the current segment's detailed content with summaries of the other segments, giving local diagnosis access to global execution context. Across Who and When and the Who and When Pro text subset, DeFA achieves the highest responsible-agent and exact step accuracy with all evaluated backbones, and the highest failure-mode accuracy among taxonomy-aligned methods on Pro. Further experiments on image and video trajectories demonstrate its applicability to multimodal failure attribution. Ablations support the contributions of segmentation, the event dependency graph, and the failure propagation graph. Using DeFA's diagnostic feedback for skill evolution in Trace2Skill improves downstream task accuracy by 6-15 percentage points over the native pipeline, showing that the diagnoses can also support agent improvement on subsequent tasks.

</details>


### [84] [Feedback Without the Wait: Piloting a Generative AI Practice Platform in a Large Maths Class](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01262)
*Lili Chen,Gavin Buskes,Yuxin Ren,Chin Tong Leong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对大型电气工程课程中即时反馈难以为继的问题，本研究设计并试点了一个生成式AI（GenAI）练习平台。该平台通过将人类监督从“实时评分”前置为“方案的事前验证”，解决了即时性与信任感之间的矛盾，旨在提供即时、脚手架式的自我指导练习反馈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管及时反馈对学生学习影响巨大，但在师生比高的大型电气工程中，学生常需等待数日才能获知错误原因。虽然生成式AI可扩展对话式反馈，但直接用于评分存在信任与问责问题，而保留人工审核又会重新引入导致反馈失效的时间延迟。这构成了即时性与人类监督可靠性之间的张力。

Method: 设计并试点一个基于GenAI的练习平台，通过事前验证解决方案而非实时评分的方式，将人类监督前置，从而在自我指导练习期间提供即时、带脚手架的反馈。

Result: 研究重点评估了学生使用该工具时的参与情况，他们对反馈价值和可靠性的感知，以及这些经验向其他工程学科转移的可能性及教训。

Conclusion: 通过将人类监督从实时环节移至事前验证阶段，该工作在实践中缓解了即时反馈需求与可信度要求之间的冲突，证明了利用GenAI在无需人工实时介入的情况下提供即时支持的可能性。

Abstract: Timely and specific feedback is one of the strongest influences on student learning, yet it is difficult to sustain in large electrical engineering classes where the ratio of students to demonstrators is high and a learner who is stuck may wait days to find out why an approach was wrong. Generative Artificial Intelligence (GenAI) offers a way to scale conversational feedback, but using it to grade assessed work raises trust and accountability concerns, and keeping a human in the loop to assure its judgements reintroduces the very delay that erodes the value of feedback. The result is a tension between the immediacy that makes feedback so impactful and the human oversight that makes it trustworthy. In this work, we set out to resolve that tension in practice by designing and piloting a GenAI practice platform that delivers immediate, scaffolded feedback during self-directed practice. This relocates human oversight from real-time grading to the upfront verification of solutions. Our goal was to understand how students engaged with the tool, how they perceived the value and reliability of its feedback, and what lessons transfer to other engineering subjects.

</details>


### [85] [SCOPE-AD: Sequential cost-aware ordinal-belief planning with energy-based models for diagnostic agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01278)
*Ziwen Yu,Ivan Koychev,Elizabeth Coulthard,Ting Zhou,Bolin Chen,Dian Hong,Zinuo You,Yujiao Wang,Anthony Mulholland,Qiang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SCOPE-AD模型，通过序贯证据获取和成本感知策略，在阿尔茨海默病（AD）诊断中实现高效、低成本的多阶段分类（CN/MCI/AD）。该模型结合能量基模型与Qwen策略蒸馏，显著优于基线方法，以极低边际成本换取微小性能提升，验证了选择性获取的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统固定模态预测器无法联合决定“获取何种测试”及“何时停止”，忽视异质性测试成本和患者负担；现有方法缺乏对有序认知衰退连续体（CN-MCI-AD）的不确定性建模及序贯决策能力。

Method: 构建SCOPE-AD框架：1) 采用掩码感知序贯模型表示不确定性；2) 利用回溯训练记录生成Bellman目标，通过能量基教师模型的行动分布蒸馏至Qwen策略模型；3) 部署时基于可用性和预算约束，动态选择证据获取或诊断动作，并在每次获取后更新证据与序贯信念。

Result: 在ADNI数据集上，SCOPE-AD平均获取成本为50.46美元时达到77.70% Macro-F1，较最强基线提升9.34个百分点；全模态评估仅提升1.89个Macro-F1点，但成本增加116.7倍。

Conclusion: 选择性证据获取可实现高效、低成本的阿尔茨海默病诊断，显著优于全模态评估的性价比。

Abstract: Alzheimer's disease (AD) diagnosis requires sequential evidence acquisition under heterogeneous test costs and patient burden. Fixed-modality predictors do not jointly decide which test to acquire or when the available evidence is sufficient for diagnosis. We propose SCOPE-AD (Sequential Cost-Aware Ordinal-Belief Planning with Energy-Based Models for Diagnostic Agents) for cost-aware classification of cognitively normal (CN), mild cognitive impairment (MCI), and AD cases. A mask-aware ordinal model represents uncertainty along the ordered CN--MCI--AD continuum. Retrospective training records provide sampled Bellman targets for an energy-based teacher, whose action distributions are distilled into a Qwen policy. At deployment, the agent selects acquisition or diagnosis actions under availability and budget constraints without access to unacquired values. After each acquisition, the evidence and ordinal belief are updated before the next decision. On ADNI, SCOPE-AD achieves 77.70\% Macro-F1 at an average acquisition cost of \$50.46, exceeding the strongest evaluated baseline by 9.34 percentage points. Full-modality evaluation raises Macro-F1 by only 1.89 points while increasing acquisition cost by 116.7 times. These results support selective acquisition for cost-effective diagnosis.

</details>


### [86] [Trustworthy Data- and ML-Ops for Intelligent Transportation Systems and Logistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01282)
*Antonio Emanuele Cinà,Giovanni Scodeller,Cecilia Caterina Pasquale,Silvia Siri,Davide Anguita,Fabio Roli,Simona Sacone,Luca Oneto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文全面综述了可信数据运营（DataOps）和机器学习运营（MLOps）在智能交通与物流（ITS&L）领域的应用，旨在提升系统效率、可靠性及决策精度，并涵盖其组件、工具、可信性方法及未来挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着智能交通与物流（ITS&L）快速发展，其对数据、人工智能（AI）和机器学习（ML）的依赖日益加深；当前文献在DataOps和MLOps的具体应用及可信性方面存在不足，亟需系统性梳理以指导研究与实践。

Method: 通过识别现有文献缺口，系统探讨DataOps和MLOps在ITS&L中的复杂性、必要性、关键组件、可用工具及实际案例；同时分析强化AI系统可信度的方法与工具，特别关注真实场景下的应用。

Result: 建立了DataOps和MLOps在ITS&L领域的理论基础与实践框架，提供了关于工具选择、实施洞察及可信性保障的综合指南，明确了该领域的关键技术路径。

Conclusion: 本研究为研究人员、行业从业者和政策制定者提供了重要资源，阐明了发展更高效、可持续且可信的智能交通与物流系统的方向，指出了持续存在的挑战及未来前景。

Abstract: The rapid evolution of Intelligent Transportation Systems and Logistics (ITS\&L) has become a cornerstone of the modern social economy, relying heavily on the integration of Data, Artificial Intelligence (AI), and, more specifically, Machine Learning (ML). This paper provides a comprehensive review of Trustworthy Data and Machine Learning Operations (DataOps and MLOps) in the ITS\&L domain, underscoring their importance in improving efficiency, reliability, and decision-making precision within transportation and logistics services. We begin by identifying gaps in current literature, offering clear context for our contribution. Subsequently, we explore the complexities of DataOps and MLOps, discussing their necessity, key components, available tools, practical insights, and case studies relevant to ITS\&L. Additionally, we address the critical issue of Trustworthiness in AI applications, examining methods and tools designed to strengthen confidence in AI systems - especially in real-world ITS\&L scenarios. The paper concludes with a discussion of persisting challenges and future prospects in this rapidly advancing field, aiming to serve as a vital resource for researchers, industry practitioners, and policy makers. Overall, this work not only establishes a foundational understanding of DataOps and MLOps in ITS\&L but also charts a path for further research and innovation in developing more efficient, sustainable, and trustworthy intelligent transportation and logistics systems.

</details>


### [87] [ITC-MoE: Importance-guided Token-aware Compression for MoE Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01296)
*Lianjun Liu,Shipeng Li,You Huang,Weiqi Yan,Mingte Qiu,Huazhong Liu,Xiaofeng Zhu,Yunshan Zhong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ITC-MoE框架，通过重要性引导的自适应Tucker压缩和令牌感知的补偿路由，解决MoE扩散语言模型中的计算存储开销问题，在保持生成质量的同时显著加速推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的低秩MoE压缩方法依赖静态分解和固定秩分配，忽视了MoE DLMs的特性：跨模态非均匀冗余和令牌级利用差异，导致无法高效压缩。

Method: 提出ITC-MoE，包含两部分：1) IATC：结合激活和梯度重要性，对专家权重进行跨模态联合分解并自适应分配秩；2) TCR：对敏感的热令牌施加低秩补偿，并为冷令牌限制候选专家集合。

Result: 在SDAR-30B-A3B-Chat-b32模型上，ITC-MoE在30%压缩预算下于MultiArith数据集保持96.33%准确率，实现最高7.22倍的端到端加速。

Conclusion: ITC-MoE通过协同调整压缩容量和推理执行以适应参数冗余和令牌变化，有效降低了MoE DLMs的成本并保持了性能。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) Diffusion Language Models (DLMs) offer flexible parallel decoding and increased model capacity, but their large number of expert parameters incurs substantial computation and storage costs. Existing low-rank MoE compression methods largely rely on static factorization and fixed rank allocation, which overlook the distinctive properties of MoE DLMs. Specifically, we identify two properties: cross-mode non-uniform redundancy, where parameter redundancy and sensitivity to rank truncation vary across the input, output, and expert modes, and token-wise utilization variation, where hot and cold tokens exhibit distinct spectral characteristics and expert activation patterns. To address these challenges, we propose ITC-MoE, an Importance-guided Token-aware Compression framework for MoE DLMs. ITC-MoE consists of two complementary components. First, Importance-guided Adaptive Tucker Compression (IATC) incorporates activation and gradient importance into expert weight transformation, jointly factorizes expert weights across multiple modes, and adaptively allocates ranks under a fixed parameter budget. Second, Token-aware Compensation and Routing (TCR) applies lightweight low-rank compensation to compression-sensitive hot tokens and restricts the candidate expert set for cold tokens with concentrated routing patterns. By jointly adapting compression capacity and inference execution to both parameter redundancy and token-wise variation, ITC-MoE substantially reduces the computation and storage costs of MoE DLMs while preserving their generation quality. For example, on SDAR-30B-A3B-Chat-b32, ITC-MoE maintains an accuracy of 96.33% on MultiArith under a 30% compression budget, while achieving up to a 7.22x end-to-end speedup. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/lianjunl13-sudo/ITC-MoE.

</details>


### [88] [Questionnaire-Guided Disaggregation of Energy Appliance Use for Domestic Smart Meter Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01297)
*Achal Nanjundamurthy,Rupam Misra,Suzanne Little,Alan F. Smeaton*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对爱尔兰30分钟分辨率智能电表数据无法捕捉家用电器使用细节的问题，提出了一种免标签（label-free）的 disaggregation 系统。该系统结合高功率负荷事件检测与问卷引导估算，将用电数据分解为9类电器，并在多个数据集上实现了最低的整体分解误差和更优的月度性能，能为家庭提供节能降本的个性化建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 爱尔兰的智能计量项目虽覆盖了超过80%的家庭，但其30分钟的记录分辨率对于计费目的足够，但对于捕捉家用电器的具体使用情况来说过于粗糙，无法满足精细化的能源管理需求。

Method: 提出一种无需标签（label-free）的非侵入式负荷分解（NILM）混合方法：
1. 对高功率负载进行事件检测；
2. 结合问卷指导的估算方法；
3. 将用电数据分解为9个电器类别。
评估使用了四个数据集：一个带商用比较器的校准家庭、两个公开基准（UK-DALE和REFIT）以及包含2,968名爱尔兰消费者4,800多年用电数据的智能电表数据集。

Result: 该方法在跨所有数据集的建筑中取得了最低的整体分解误差，并且在54对月度数据上表现出显著更好的性能（p<0.001，经Holm校正），优于两个独立开发的分解系统。

Conclusion: 该混合方法不仅提高了非侵入式负荷分解的准确性，还能为家庭用户提供关于其能耗模式的有用建议，以及如何减少或转移某些电器用电量以降低成本的具体策略。

Abstract: Ireland's smart metering programme records electricity use at 30-minute resolution, with smart meters installed in over 80\% of households as of late 2025. While this is useful for billing of smart, time-of-use tariffs, it is too coarse to capture use of domestic appliances. We present a label-free disaggregation system that breaks usage data into 9 appliance categories by combining event detection for high-power loads with questionnaire-guided estimation. Our evaluation draws on four datasets: a calibration household with a commercial comparator, two public benchmarks (UK-DALE and REFIT) with per-appliance sub-metering, and a smart meter dataset of more than 4,800 years of use from 2,968 Irish consumers. Compared against two independently developed disaggregation systems our hybrid method combining analysis of usage data with questionnaire results, achieves the lowest whole-decomposition error on all buildings across the datasets, with better month-level performance over 54 paired months ($p<0.001$, Holm-corrected). Our method provides useful advice on a household's energy consumption patterns and advice on how to reduce or shift usage on some appliances in order to reduce costs.

</details>


### [89] [DAYJOB: A Benchmark for Long-Horizon Professional Work](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01306)
*Stephanie Finley,Liudas Panavas,Thomas Mikkelson,Cam Hinton,Stacey Ganss,Bradley Monton,Emily Kendall,Michelle Spradlin,Lydia Bye,Michael O'Brien,Lauren Ylvisaker,Derek Ray,Suhaas Garre,Sushant Mehta,Edwin Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 引入包含130个专业任务的DAYJOB基准（医疗50个，金融80个），使用Harbor容器和专家二值标准评估AI智能体。最强模型Claude Opus 5.5通过率仅约24%，中位配置通过率低，表明专业工作场景对AI仍有巨大挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专业工作通常始于模糊请求，需自主判断需求、文档及前提假设。现有评测缺乏对此类复杂、长周期专业任务的量化评估，旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 构建DAYJOB基准：由专业人士在医疗和金融领域创建130个任务，平均耗时13.6至16.6小时/任务。采用容器化Harbor环境运行，每个任务包含二元标准专家评测规则。通过聚合不同开发者的30种模型配置进行盲测，并记录成功率和案例分析。

Result: 在30种模型配置中，最强模型Claude Opus 5.5在医疗任务中通过率为24.7%，金融任务为23.9%；中位配置的通过率分别为0.6%和2.5%。案例分析显示，智能体常接受与记录相矛盾的前提，或在分析一致的情况下输入错误数据。

Conclusion: 当前大模型在处理需长期推理、严格合规和专业判断的高难度真实世界任务时表现仍显不足，存在事实违背和逻辑断裂问题，专业级AI代理能力尚未成熟。

Abstract: Professional work often starts with a brief request that leaves the professional to work out what is needed, which documents matter, and whether the request's premise holds. We introduce DAYJOB, a benchmark of 130 tasks built by professionals in healthcare (50) and finance (80). The tasks are estimated to take a professional 13.6 hours on average in healthcare and 16.6 in finance. Each task is a containerized Harbor environment with an expert rubric of binary criteria (median 47.5 and 57.5 per task) that an agentic judge applies to the delivered files, and an attempt passes only if it meets every criterion. Across 30 model configurations from 13 developers, the strongest, Claude Opus 5.5, passes 24.7% of healthcare and 23.9% of finance attempts, and the median configuration passes 0.6% and 2.5%. In case studies, agents accept premises that the record contradicts and carry wrong inputs through otherwise consistent analyses. We release all healthcare tasks, 50 of the 80 finance tasks, the evaluation harness, and the leaderboard.

</details>


### [90] [ProtoFlow: Prototype-Guided Flow Matching for Multivariate Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01320)
*Shibo Feng,Wanjin Feng,Yang Qiu,Deheng Ye,Peilin Zhao,Chunyan Miao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ProtoFlow框架，结合向量量化（VQ）与基于原型先验的流匹配（Flow Matching），解决传统自回归方法存在的曝光偏差问题，实现高效且高精度的多维时间序列预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩散模型推理步骤多、效率低；VAE基的非迭代框架中，基于向量量化的方法通常依赖自回归（AR）生成，导致曝光偏差和训练-推断不匹配；而现有的流匹配方法通常使用通用高斯先初始传输，缺乏对潜在结构的利用。

Method: 提出ProtoFlow：1. 将多维序列映射到离散潜在空间（通过VQ）；2. 利用训练好的VQ码本构建结构化原型先验；3. 基于DiT架构学习整流流（Rectified Flow），将样本从该原型先验条件性地传输到未来潜在表示，而非从零噪声开始。

Result: 在基准数据集上，ProtoFlow避免了自回归令牌预测的 rollout 不匹配，促进了更快的训练收敛，并在保持高效推理的同时实现了 Superior（优越）的预测性能。

Conclusion: 利用VQ码本作为流匹配的 Informative Prior（信息先验）是可行的且有效的，该方法为非自回归的多维时间序列预测提供了更优的性能与效率平衡。

Abstract: Generative modeling has shown strong promise for multivariate time mseries (MTS) forecasting, especially scale to high-dimensional settings. Diffusion-based methods achieve competitive performance but typically require many sampling steps at inference. VAE-based non-iterative forecasting frameworks have therefore emerged as an efficient alternative. Within this line of work, vector quantization (VQ) enables controllable latent space modeling by mapping multivariate series into compact discrete representations. Existing VQ-based forecasting methods, however, typically rely on autoregressive (AR) token generation, which suffers from exposure bias and training-inference mismatch. Flow matching provides an efficient non-autoregressive alternative for latent forecasting, but existing formulations usually initialize transport from a generic Gaussian prior. We instead observe that the trained VQ codebook already captures representative latent prototypes and can thus serve as a more informative prior for flow matching. Based on this insight, we propose ProtoFlow, a forecasting framework that combines vector-quantized autoencoding with Prototype-prior Flow matching. Our method first maps multivariate sequences into a discrete latent space, then constructs a structured prior from the learned codebook, and finally learns a DiT-based rectified flow to transport samples from this prior to future latent representations conditioned on historical observations. By replacing generic noise initialization with a learned prototype prior, ProtoFlow avoids the rollout mismatch of AR token prediction and promotes faster training convergence. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets show that it consistently achieves superior forecasting performance with efficient inference.

</details>


### [91] [TRACE: Trajectory Return Attribution and Contrastive Erasure for Multi-Turn Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01323)
*Fengpeng Li,Kemou Li,Qizhou Wang,Haiwei Wu,Jiantao Zhou,Di Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出TRACE方法，通过token级对比擦除目标解决大语言模型在多轮交互中的安全漏洞，显著降低攻击成功率同时保持模型效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的安全对齐LLM在单轮上下文中抑制有害请求有效，但目标分散到多轮时容易失效；偏好目标的训练损失无法控制未见历史轨迹的风险。

Method: TRACE（Trajectory Return Attribution and Contrastive Erasure）：基于监督单轮抑制对多轮风险有界性的分析，构建token级目标。通过在拒绝响应上加权折扣返回的优势，并利用冻结参考模型与拒绝消融副本的对比，为早期token分配后续拒绝证据的信用；在高间隙位置结合观测token与策略替代方案进行擦除，并用梯度范数惩罚替代保留集。

Result: 在5个开源模型和7种多轮攻击中，TRACE在所有35个模型-攻击对中实现了最低的攻击成功率（ASR），且模型效用（MMLU和HellaSwag指标）下降不超过1.23分。

Conclusion: TRACE通过将多轮风险边界原理转化为具体的token级训练目标，有效提升了LLM在多轮对抗场景下的安全性，且几乎无损于模型原有能力。

Abstract: Safety-aligned large language models (LLMs) often refuse a harmful request but comply once the same goal is spread over several turns. Preference objectives score whole responses to single prompts, so their training loss alone cannot control risk on unseen histories. Our analysis gives sufficient conditions under which suppression at supervised single-turn contexts yields a bound on multi-turn trajectory risk. The bound accounts for coverage, transfer slack, and leakage, and characterizes contraction relative to a base-policy risk budget evaluated on the trained policy's contexts. TRACE (Trajectory Return Attribution and Contrastive Erasure) turns this principle into a token-level objective. On the safe response, each token is weighted by the discounted return of a refusal-attributable advantage. The advantage compares a frozen reference model with its refusal-ablated copy, allowing earlier response tokens to receive credit from later refusal-related evidence. At high-gap positions on rejected responses, TRACE combines the observed token with policy-selected alternatives in the erasure target. A gradient-norm penalty replaces the retain set. Across five open-weight models and seven multi-turn attacks, TRACE gives the lowest attack success rate (ASR) in all 35 model and attack pairs, while the model utility evaluated on MMLU and HellaSwag drop by at most 1\.23 points. Source code can be found in the supplemental material.

</details>


### [92] [PPO-HRAP: Proximal Policy Optimization with a Hybrid Regime-Aware Policy for Risk-Controlled Trading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01325)
*Duong Hien Chi Kien,Thanh Trung Huynh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PPO-HRAP算法，结合PPO与可解释的市场状态先验，有效平衡收益与回撤控制。在SPY上实现夏普比率0.6447，最大回撤降至18.47%，优于基准且具单资产泛化潜力，但存在高换手率及跨资产稳健性不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习交易常面临收益参与与回撤控制的失衡：纯利润策略易陷入被动多头暴露，而过度风险惩罚策略则导致市场波动时过于保守。旨在解决这一两难困境，提升风险调整后收益。

Method: 构建混合机制感知策略（PPO-HRAP），融合近端策略优化（PPO）与市场状态先验。智能体观测市场特征与投资组合状态，奖励函数包含对数回报、VIX条件下的回撤增加惩罚、目标偏离度及换手成本。动作输出为PPOActor输出与市场状态推导的目标敞口的混合值。

Result: 在2020-2022年SPY测试期，总收益27.62%，年化收益8.48%，Sharpe比率0.6447，Sortino比率0.8588，Calmar比率0.4592；最大回撤从买入持有的34.10%降至18.47%。五种子代实验显示收益(0.2725±0.0109)和夏普比率(0.6219±0.0565)稳定。在QQQ和DIA的单次跨资产测试中，均获得总收益和夏普比率第一。

Conclusion: 将学习到的动作与波动性感知的状态先验相结合，是改善风险调整交易行为的实用方法。然而，当前策略仍存在高换手成本问题，且除SPY外的跨资产稳健性目前仅基于单次运行证据，需进一步验证。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning for trading often struggles to balance upside participation with drawdown control. Profit-only policies can collapse toward passive long exposure on upward-drifting assets, while aggressively risk-penalized rewards can become too defensive during volatile periods. This paper proposes PPO-HRAP, a hybrid regime-aware policy that combines Proximal Policy Optimization with an interpretable regime prior. The agent observes both market features and portfolio-state variables, receives a reward combining portfolio log return, VIX-conditioned drawdown-increase penalty, target-exposure deviation, and turnover cost, and executes a blended action between the PPO actor output and a regime-derived target exposure. On the held-out 2020-2022 SPY test window, PPO-HRAP achieves 27.62% total return, 8.48% annualized return, 0.6447 Sharpe ratio, 0.8588 Sortino ratio, and 0.4592 Calmar ratio, while reducing maximum drawdown from 34.10% for Buy and Hold to 18.47%. Across five SPY seeds, PPO-HRAP remains stable with mean total return $0.2725 \pm 0.0109$ and mean Sharpe ratio $0.6219 \pm 0.0565$. Single-run cross-asset tests on QQQ and DIA further show that the proposed method ranks first on total return and Sharpe ratio for all three reported assets. These results suggest that blending learned actions with a volatility-aware regime prior is a practical way to improve risk-adjusted trading behavior, although the current policy still incurs high turnover and cross-asset robustness beyond SPY remains limited to single-run evidence.

</details>


### [93] [An ontology for cross-sectoral crisis management: core and public health modules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01326)
*Aldo Gangemi,Rita T. Sousa,Luigi Asprino,Giorgia Lodi,Andrea G. Nuzzolese,Valentina Presutti,Johannes Gysen,Diana F. Sousa,Luigi Spagnolo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文介绍了欧洲危机管理本体（ECMO），一个面向灾害风险管理和响应的模块化OWL本体。通过核心模块解决概念歧义，并包含特定领域子模块（如公共卫生）。该本体成功用于将非结构化流行病学新闻转换为结构化的知识图谱，证明了其在统一数据表示和集成中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为解决灾难风险管理中跨部门、多源异构数据的语义互操作性问题，提供一个通用的参考框架以应对危险类型与事件表现之间的歧义。

Method: 1. 构建基于OWL的模块化网络架构；2. ECMO-CORE模块利用本体设计模式和OWL2 punning技术解析核心概念（如危险、事件、影响）；3. 开发领域特定模块（如对齐全ICD-11和SNOMED CT的公共卫生模块）；4. 应用本体现有数据：使用联合研究中心开放来源流行病情报系统数据，构建从非结构化新闻到合规知识图谱的端到端管道。

Result: ECMO能够作为正式护栏，确保跨领域知识的清晰性和一致性。通过实际案例演示，验证了从非结构化文本中提取信息并生成符合ECMO标准的知识图谱的可行性。

Conclusion: ECMO提供了必要的形式化约束，实现了灾难管理领域的统一知识表示与集成，支持跨部门的数据共享与分析。本体已公开可用。

Abstract: This paper presents the European Crisis Management Ontology (ECMO), a modular OWL-based ontology intended as a cross-sectoral reference for disaster risk reduction and response. ECMO is designed to be organised as a network of ontological modules. Among the modules, ECMO-CORE captures fundamental crisis management concepts such as hazard, event, exposure, impact, and response measure and uses ontology design patterns and the OWL2 punning technique to resolve ambiguities between hazard types and event manifestations. In addition, domain-specific modules are defined as in the case of the public health module aligned with SNOMED CT and ICD-11. To demonstrate the resource's utility, we used ECMO to represent the data of the Epidemic Intelligence from Open Sources system of the Joint Research Centre to generate an end-to-end pipeline that populates an ECMO-compliant knowledge graph from unstructured epidemiological news. Initial results demonstrate that ECMO provides the formal guardrails necessary for consistent and unified knowledge representation and integration. The ontology is publicly available at https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.20070268 and is released under the Creative Commons Attribution 4.0 International (CC BY 4.0) license.

</details>


### [94] [Verify Claims, Not Scores: Evidence-Based Verification of Modular Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01348)
*Ali Atiah Alzahrani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对模块化智能体的声明级验证审计协议，通过评估证据而非整体任务得分，来诊断组件变更的影响及改进潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的开发评估方法通常依赖聚合任务得分，无法区分改进是否可达、具体哪个组件导致价值损失，或验证器自身的认证有效性。

Method: 引入声明级验证审计，为每个结论记录证据并给出四种判决（支持、不支持、未决、未评估）。使用三个工具提供证据：Oracle策略评估在特定动作集下的理论改进上限；替换单组件为理想对应物以定位价值损失；独立测试验证评分器是否能准确界定其试图度量的量。

Result: 在合成市场的受限投资组合分配智能体中应用发现：完美状态信息值的评估依赖于所用的动作集；场景生成器丢弃了大量状态信号，而提升局部保真度并未改善决策；运行时验证器可被绕过且不影响可见结果。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于审计协议及其强制执行的证据区分机制；实证发现具有特定情境依赖性，不具普适性。

Abstract: When developers change one component of an agent, such as its controller, a learned model or its verifier, they usually judge the change by an aggregate task score. That score cannot tell whether improvement was attainable, which component lost value, or what the agent's own checks certify. We introduce a claim-specific verification audit for modular agents that plan, act, check and refine. Instead of scoring the agent, the audit scores the evidence: each conclusion is recorded with the evidence behind it, one of four verdicts (supported, unsupported, unresolved or not evaluated) and the boundary within which it holds. Three tools supply that evidence. Oracle policies measure attainable improvement under an explicitly stated action set, so that a low value can be traced to the evaluation rather than to the environment. Replacing one component at a time with a perfect counterpart locates lost value, with null results read as unresolved whenever a downstream component could mask them. A separate test asks whether the verifier's score identifies the quantity it is read as bounding. Applied to a constrained portfolio-allocation agent in a synthetic market with known hidden regimes, the audit shows that the value of perfect regime information depends on the action set used to measure it, that the scenario generator discards most of the regime signal while better local fidelity does not improve decisions, and that the runtime verifier can be bypassed with no visible change in outcomes. The contribution is the protocol and the evidential distinctions it enforces; the empirical findings are specific to the agent and environment studied.

</details>


### [95] [Generation Provenance Before Behavior Attribution: Auditing Synthetic Speech Research Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01378)
*Sidi Chang,Peiying Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种合成数据生成溯源机制（generation-provenance substrate），以解决行为归因难题，通过日本护理交接管道进行审计，证明溯源是必要但不充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统波形-标签对无法保留训练项的生产知识，导致难以将模型行为准确归因于具体的合成训练数据。

Method: 构建绑定源规范、生成内容、波形、目标、事实需求、质量信号及不可变标识的合成研究对象；在日本私人护理交接流程中进行审计，分析113个资产、1.552小时合成语音的审核案例。

Result: 实现了场景种子不相交且不可变版本化的忠实清单，但上游精确归因受限于浮动生成器别名、缺失每片段TTS/代码戳记及未版本化的检查提示。

Conclusion: 生成溯源仅定义因果图候选和审计单元，贡献性归因仍需冻结训练运行及干预证据；提供了紧凑溯源合约、审计协议及受限案例研究，不宣称因果归因或临床有效性。

Abstract: Attributing model behavior to synthetic training data requires knowing what produced each training item before estimating what that item caused. A waveform-label pair does not preserve this knowledge. We propose a generation-provenance substrate in which a synthetic research object binds source specification, generated content, waveform, target, fact requirements, quality signals, review lineage, and immutable manifest identity. Producer and selection mechanism determine evidentiary meaning; storage location and variable name do not. We audit this substrate in a private Japanese care-handoff pipeline. A 113-asset review population contains 1.552 hours of synthetic speech across six scenario families; all items have linked audio, transcripts, candidate notes, and fact checklists, but human evidence is selective and source-specific. Two faithful-only manifests are scenario-seed-disjoint and immutably versioned, while exact upstream attribution remains blocked by floating generator aliases, missing per-clip TTS and code stamps, and an unversioned checking prompt. We argue that generation provenance is necessary but not sufficient for behavior attribution: it defines the candidate causal graph and audit units, whereas contributive attribution still requires frozen training runs and intervention or influence evidence. The paper contributes a compact provenance contract, an audit protocol, and a bounded case study for synthetic-data attribution; controlled research access may be offered, but we do not claim causal training-data attribution, clinical validity, or unrestricted public release.

</details>


### [96] [Gacha Decoding: Eliciting Diverse Generations Through Instruction Following](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01382)
*Scott Geng,Yufei Zhang,Joseph Lee,Jerry Li,Marjan Ghazvininejad,Pang Wei Koh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Gacha Decoding，一种在推理时利用指令跟随能力结合外部随机性来提升语言模型生成多样性的新方法。该方法在保持质量不变的情况下，以少得多的样本量实现了更高的多样性，且随模型能力增强而效果更佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决语言模型生成中多样性与能力/熵值之间的长期矛盾。传统方法依赖Token值，但往往随着模型能力提升或采用现有多样性技术后，多样性反而下降或效率低下。旨在探索不依赖内部熵值的新型多样化生成机制。

Method: 将多样性生成视为指令跟随问题。不依赖语言模型的Token熵，而是结合其指令遵循能力和外部随机数生成器（RNG）工具（即“带骰子的规划”），通过外部随机性引导模型识别并实现响应空间中的不同模式。

Result: 1. 在开放域任务（聊天、创意写作、图像生成规划、蛋白质设计）中显著优于现有方法，多样性达到次优方法的2.4倍（Vendi评分）。2. 发现高质模式的样本量减少11倍。3. 能发现其他方法无法揭示的新颖模式。4. 随着基础模型指令跟随能力提升，多样性持续改善，与传统方法中多样性随熵值降低而恶化的趋势相反。

Conclusion: 语言生成的多样性主要由指令跟随能力驱动，而非仅由Token熵决定。Gacha Decoding证明了通过结合外部随机性与指令遵循能力，可以逆转多样性与模型能力之间的张力，实现高效且可扩展的多样化生成。

Abstract: We introduce Gacha Decoding, an inference-time method for eliciting diverse language model generations that scales with model capability. Across open-ended domains (in-the-wild chat, creative writing, planning for image generation, and protein design), Gacha Decoding significantly outperforms existing generation diversity approaches at equal quality (up to 2.4x Vendi over the next-best prior approach), reaching the same number of high-quality modes with over an order of magnitude fewer samples (11.0x) and discovering novel modes that no other approach surfaces. Our key insight is to treat diversity as an instruction-following problem: rather than relying on the LM's token entropy, we combine its instruction-following capability with randomness from an external RNG tool to scalably identify and realize distinct modes of the response space. This approach of "planning with dice" enables Gacha to invert the long-observed tension between diversity and model capability. As the underlying LM becomes a better instruction follower, diversity under Gacha Decoding consistently improves--even as its token entropy and diversity under prior approaches decline. Together, our results highlight that instruction following, rather than token entropy alone, can drive generation diversity.

</details>


### [97] [PRISM: A Category-Theoretic Framework for Measuring and Refining Multimodal Analogies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01383)
*Mirella Zeisler,Ojas Shirekar,Mircea Licǎ,Chirag Raman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PRISM框架，通过类别理论构建显式关系映射，利用“拉回分数”量化并迭代优化多模态类比中的关系一致性，在视觉隐喻生成任务上显著提升了类比恰当性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI驱动的多模态类比生成方法缺乏可解释的度量标准，无法有效验证生成的输出是否真正理解并保持了跨领域间的关系结构。

Method: 提出模态无关的PRISM框架：1）基于类别理论将类比表示为显式关系映射；2）引入“拉回分数”（pullback score）量化图形表示中的关系对齐程度；3）构建迭代细化循环，利用拉回分数作为上下文反馈信号指导VLMs修正生成图像，以提升关系深度。

Result: 在AnaloBench基准测试中，仅凭拉回分数选择正确类比的准确率达82.5%；人类评估显示，经PRISM细化的图像在隐喻一致性和类比恰当性上优于零样本生成，且在57.65%的双向比较中被参与者偏好。

Conclusion: PRSM有效提升了多模态类比生成的关系质量，但定性分析表明过度依赖分数可能导致生成偏向视觉拥挤的组合，而非真正的深层关系对应，揭示了当前量化指标在捕捉深层语义上的局限性。

Abstract: Analogical reasoning involves identifying and preserving relational structures across domains. However, existing approaches to AI-driven multimodal analogy generation lack an interpretable measure of whether this structure is understood and maintained in the generated output. We address this gap with Pullback Refinement via Interpretable Structural Mapping (PRISM), a modality-agnostic framework for measuring and improving relational alignment in multimodal analogies, evaluated on visual metaphor generation. PRISM represents analogies as explicit relational mappings grounded in category theory and uses VLMs to instantiate these structures across modalities. Its first component, the pullback score, quantifies relational alignment from the resulting graph representation. On the AnaloBench benchmark, selecting the correct analogy purely by pullback score achieves 82.5% accuracy, demonstrating that the score captures meaningful relational information. PRISM's second component is an iterative refinement loop that uses the pullback score as an in-context feedback signal to iteratively revise the generated image towards greater relational depth. VLM-as-a-judge and human evaluations show that PRISM consistently improves metaphor consistency and analogy appropriateness over zero- shot generation, with human participants preferring the refined output in 57.65% of pairwise comparisons. However, a qualitative analysis reveals that refinement can favour visually crowded compositions rather than genuinely deeper relational correspondences.

</details>


### [98] [AiSearch: Interactive Multi-Modal Search with VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01389)
*Ali Koksal,Mei Chee Leong,Vicky Sintunata,Ching Ling Chin,Wee Teck Fong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AiSearch，一个利用VLMs零样本能力的多模态检索框架，支持实时自然语言搜索、基于用户反馈的交互式优化及跨模型视觉基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代检索系统需兼顾自动化与交互性，使用户能实时搜索并精炼结果，同时需要评估不同视觉语言模型（VLMs）在特定任务中的适用性。

Method: 构建AiSearch框架，利用VLMs的自然语言理解能力实现图像和视频的自然语言检索；通过集成用户反馈机制实现搜索结果的交互式微调；提供多VLMs可视化的基准测试以辅助模型选择。

Result: 成功实现了灵活的多模态检索流程，支持自然语言输入、实时用户反馈迭代优化以及直观的模型性能对比基准。

Conclusion: AiSearch有效结合了VLMs的强大泛化能力与交互式 refinement 机制，为复杂检索任务提供了高效且用户友好的解决方案，并促进了多模型的适配选择。

Abstract: Modern retrieval systems must both be automated and interactive, allowing users to search and refine results in real time. We present AiSearch, a flexible multimodal retrieval framework that leverages the zero shot capabilities of Vision Language Models (VLMs) for natural language search over images and videos. AiSearch supports interactive search refinement through user feedback to tailor results to the user's intent, and allows visual benchmarking across multiple VLMs, enabling users to select the most suitable model for their task.

</details>


### [99] [Contrastive Attention Mitigates Spectral Bias in Spiking Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01403)
*Xiaoli Liu,Malu Zhang,Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文揭示了脉冲自注意力（SSA）作为低通滤波器导致高频信息丢失是Spiking Transformers性能低于ANN的主因，并提出Spiking Contrastive Attention (SCA)模块，通过全局对比聚合和局部微分细化增强高频信息，在多种任务中显著提升性能并降低复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Spiking Transformers结合了SNN的能量效率和自注意力的表示能力，但其性能仍明显落后于传统人工神经网络（ANN）。既往研究多归因于二进制激活问题，但本文旨在揭示更深层的频谱特性缺陷，并解决由此产生的性能差距。

Method: 1. 通过多尺度频谱分析，发现脉冲神经元和脉冲自注意力（SSA均充当低通滤波器，导致高频分量耗散；2. 提出Spiking Contrastive Attention (SCA)范式，受生物视觉系统边缘检测和差分感知特性启发；3. 通过全局对比聚合提取对比原型，并结合局部微分细化来增强高频信息。

Result: 1. SCA是一个通用模块，在图像分类、语义分割和基于事件的跟踪任务中 consistently 提升了Spiking Transformer的性能；2. 相比原始SSA，SCA实现了更低的复杂度，提供了更高的效率；3. 实验验证了其在增强高频信息和提升模型效能方面的有效性。

Conclusion: ScA有效解决了Spiking Transformers中因低频滤波效应导致的性能瓶颈，确立了其作为构建高效能、低功耗Spiking Transformers基本模块的潜力。

Abstract: Spiking Transformers merge the energy-efficiency of spiking neural networks (SNNs) with the representational power of self-attention, creating a promising architecture for high-performance, energy-efficient computation. However, a performance gap persists versus its counterparts in artificial neural networks (ANNs). Unlike prior works attributing this to binary activations, we reveal that both spiking neurons and spiking self-attention (SSA) act as low-pass filters through multiscale spectral analysis. This characteristic leads to the dissipation of high-frequency components. To address this issue, we propose the Spiking Contrastive Attention (SCA) paradigm, which draw inspiration from the edge-detection and differential sensing properties of biological visual system. By extracting contrast prototypes via global contrastive aggregation and applying local differential refinement, SCA effectively enhances high-frequency information. Extensive experiments show that SCA is a general module that consistently boosts Spiking Transformers across image classification, semantic segmentation, and event-based tracking. Furthermore, it achieves lower complexity, offering superior efficiency over original SSA. These results establish its potential as a fundamental building block for energy-efficient Spiking Transformers.

</details>


### [100] [Beyond Memory: Harnessing Long-Horizon Agents with Explicit Belief States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01415)
*Yu Luo,Jiamin Jiang,Yimin Zuo,Xidao Wen,Rongchen Gao,Yongqian Sun,Shenglin Zhang,Guiyang Liu,Cheng Zhang,Fang Situ,Qi Zhou,Dan Pei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PoS框架，通过显式构建和维护信念状态来解决LLM智能体在复杂任务中上下文管理一致性问题，有效防止“信念陷阱”并提升长程任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体组织交互历史的方式无法确保对当前世界的连贯理解，难以有效管理复杂任务中的上下文。

Method: 引入推理时框架PoS，构建包含世界状态估计和未解决任务需求的显式信念状态；通过一致性验证和任务进度监控检测“信念陷阱”，并根据陷阱模式和任务类型进行定制化恢复。

Result: 在执行和诊断的四个基准测试上，基于三种LLM backbone均取得最高整体性能；消融实验证实一致性验证和恢复机制的重要性，上下文扩展实验显示其对上下文增长的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 信念的显式构建与持续维护是超越简单历史保留和压缩、实现长期上下文管理的基础。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents can now undertake increasingly complex tasks, but the way they organize interaction history into memory does not ensure a coherent understanding of the current world. We introduce PoS, an inference-time framework that constructs and continually maintains explicit belief states as the agent's decision context. Each belief combines an estimate of the current world state with unresolved task requirements, making explicit what the agent still needs to learn and accomplish. To keep this belief reliable and actionable, PoS validates its consistency and monitors task progress to detect Belief Trapping, where the agent continues to act without making meaningful progress toward the goal. Recovery is then tailored to both the trapping pattern and the type of unresolved task requirement. Experiments on four benchmarks spanning execution and diagnosis show that PoS achieves the highest overall performance on every benchmark with all three LLM backbones. Ablations demonstrate the importance of consistency validation and recovery, while context-scaling experiments show resilience to context growth. Together, these results support belief construction and continual maintenance as a foundation for long-horizon context management beyond history retention and compression.

</details>


### [101] [SpikeMoE: Brain-Inspired Competitive Routing for Flexible Spiking Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01418)
*Xiaoli Liu,Yujie Liang,Jialin Li,Malu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SpikeMoE框架，结合脉冲神经网络（SNN）与混合专家模型（MoE），利用基于尖峰的k-WTA路由器和两阶段缺失模态建模模块，在保持高能效的同时实现了视觉、语言和跨模态任务的最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将具有生物启发动态的SNN与执行条件计算的MoE相结合，旨在构建灵活的神经架构以平衡性能与能效。关键挑战在于设计基于脉冲活动的专家选择机制，以及解决多模态任务中不完整的输入问题。

Method: 1. 设计受海马CA1区域抑制竞争启发的基于尖峰的k-WTA路由器（Spike-based k-WTA Router），通过侧向抑制和不应期根据离散脉冲计数选择Top-K专家。
2. 构建SpikeMoE框架整合神经元尺度动态与模型尺度专家选择。
3. 引入两阶段缺失模态建模模块，结合观察到的模态原型池和经验可学习嵌入来重建缺失模态表示。

Result: 在视觉、语言和跨模态基准测试中，SpikeMoE在SNN基线中达到最先进（SOTA）性能，并匹配或超过对应的人工神经网络（ANN）性能；同时在多种缺失模态条件下保持鲁棒性，验证了高性能与高能效之间的良好权衡。

Conclusion: 成功将脉冲动态与稀疏专家计算相结合，证明了其作为高效脑启发计算方法的潜力，为未来的低功耗智能系统提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) enable event-driven computation through biologically inspired dynamics at the neuronal scale, while Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) perform conditional computation through expert selection at the model scale. Integrating their strengths offers potential for flexible neural architectures. A key challenge, however, lies in designing an expert selection mechanism based on spiking activity. To address this, we introduce a spike-based k-WTA Router inspired by competition-inhibition observed in the hippocampal CA1 region. The router incorporates lateral inhibition and refractory period to select Top-K experts according to discrete spike counts. Building on this, we present SpikeMoE, a framework that integrates neuronal-scale spiking dynamics with model-scale expert selection. To address incomplete multisensory inputs in multimodal tasks, we further equip SpikeMoE with a two-stage missing-modality modeling module that combines empirical prototypes from an observed-modality pool with modality-specific learnable embeddings to construct missing-modality representations. Experiments on vision, language, and multimodal benchmarks demonstrate that SpikeMoE achieves state-of-the-art performance among the SNN baselines, matches or exceeds the performance of ANN counterparts, and maintains robustness across diverse missing-modality conditions. These results demonstrate a favorable trade-off between performance and energy efficiency, validating the integration of spiking dynamics with sparse expert computation and highlighting SpikeMoE as a promising approach to energy-efficient brain-inspired computing.

</details>


### [102] [A Deterministic and Auditable AI Security Risk Assessment Framework with ATLAS Aligned Executable Rules and Formal Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01436)
*Yixuan Huang,Basel Halak,Boojoong Kang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于证据的AI安全评估框架，将评估转化为确定性决策函数。通过标准化工程工件为MITRE ATLAS控制ID，输出可追溯的技术可行性与影响评分，并经过形式化验证以确保语义正确性，实现了评估的可复现性和机器可执行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI安全评估方法多依赖叙述性清单或评估者打分，难以在审计下复现和辩护，缺乏从可见工程工件到稳定技术结果层面的显式、机器可评估的映射关系。

Method: 1. 构建确定性决策函数框架；2. 将异构工程工件规范化为项目独立的Control ID分类法，采用四级序数评分；3. 基于MITRE ATLAS快照，通过明确的缓解-控制映射编译技术层谓词；4. 封装规范选择为版本化的评估策略对象以支持可重复重评；5. 对编译的评价器进行形式化验证（有界性、完全性、有序语义一致性和单调性）。

Result: 在五个公开开源AI项目上评估，结果显示在加强可观察控制后，可行性剖面出现一致的向下偏移（风险降低）；然而，当技术特定的核心控制措施缺失时，最坏情况下的剩余可行性仍然存在。框架还验证了对软件物料清单（SBOM）生成和CI安全扫描门等真实工程变更的响应能力。

Conclusion: 该框架通过将评估操作化为确定性的、带有可追溯证据链接的机制，解决了现有评估方法不可复现的问题，并证明了其能有效量化安全加固干预的效果，尽管核心控制的缺失仍是主要风险源。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence systems are increasingly deployed in high impact and safety critical settings, yet security assessment remains difficult to reproduce and defend under audit. Existing approaches often rely on narrative checklists or assessor driven scoring, and they lack an explicit, machine evaluable mapping from observable engineering artefacts to stable technique level outcomes. We present an evidence driven AI security assessment framework that operationalises assessment as a deterministic decision function. The framework normalises heterogeneous artefacts into a project independent Control ID taxonomy scored on a bounded four level ordinal scale, compiles technique level predicates from a pinned MITRE ATLAS snapshot via an explicit mitigation to control mapping, and outputs technique indexed feasibility and impact levels with traceable links back to the triggering evidence. We package all normative choices as a versioned assessment policy object to support repeatable reassessment across snapshots. To ensure semantic correctness, we formally verify boundedness, totality, ordered semantic consistency, and monotonicity of the compiled evaluator over the full declared score domain. We evaluate the framework on five public open source AI projects pinned to explicit repository snapshots, quantify before and after changes under a unified hardening intervention, and validate responsiveness to real engineering changes through fork based implementations of Software Bill of Materials (SBOM) generation and Continuous integration (CI) security scanning gates. Results show consistent downward shifts in feasibility profiles under strengthened observable controls, while worst case residual feasibility persists when technique specific core controls remain absent from the evidence scope.

</details>


### [103] [DRelay: Global Draft Context for Prefix-Aware Parallel Speculative Decoding Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01439)
*Zhuoyu Wang,Junnan Huang,Xinyu Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DRelay，通过全局信息对候选token进行前缀感知的选择性修复，解决并行草稿中早期选择错误导致的接受前缀长度受限问题，显著提升LLM投机解码的加速比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有并行草稿方法受限于“接受前缀长度”，单次早期选择错误会导致后续预测失效，限制了投机解码的效率增益。

Method: 1. 构建全局读者（global reader）提取每个候选在整个草稿块中的预测信息；2. 设计因果选择器（causal selector），结合全局信息与历史选择路径，判断当前原位选择是否与全局证据一致；3. 根据判断结果保留或替换token以修复早期错误；4. 联合训练草稿主干网与选择器，引入按位置加权修复损失。

Result: 在8个基准测试上，DRelay的平均接受长度和端到端解码性能均优于DFlash、Domino和DSpark。在SGLang服务下，其端到端加速比特于这三种基线分别提升了14.7%-16.8%、8.7%-9.3%和8.1%-9.3%。

Conclusion: DRelay通过前缀感知的选择性修复机制有效延长了接受前缀，显著提升了大型语言模型投机解码的实际推理速度。

Abstract: Parallel drafting reduces the drafting overhead of speculative decoding for large language models (LLMs), but its gains remain limited by the accepted prefix length. Even when the correct token is present in the candidate pool, a single early selection error prevents subsequent predictions from being used. We propose DRelay, which uses global information from the entire draft block to perform prefix-aware selective repair of candidate selections before target-model verification. DRelay bases its decisions on candidate correlations and the selected path: a global reader extracts predictive information across positions for each candidate. While a causal selector combines candidate-level information extracted by the global read with the tokens selected at preceding positions to determine whether the native choice at the current position is consistent with the global evidence and the selected prefix. It then decides whether to retain or replace the token, thereby repairing early errors and extending the accepted prefix. We further jointly train the draft backbone and the selector, combining candidate-support learning with a repair objective, while weighting the repair loss according to each block position's potential contribution to the consecutive accepted prefix. Across eight diverse benchmarks on an H800 GPU, DRelay consistently improves both average acceptance length and end-to-end decoding performance over DFlash, Domino, and DSpark. Under SGLang serving, DRelay improves average end-to-end speedup over DFlash, Domino, and DSpark by 14.7%-16.8%, 8.7%-9.3%, and 8.1%-9.3%, respectively.

</details>


### [104] [A Multi-Agent LLM Framework for Personalized Health Checkup Interpretation and Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01451)
*HyungJun Kim,Taehan Lee,Soojin Cheon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种多智能体大语言模型（LLM）系统，用于健康检查结果的个性化解读。该系统通过识别意图并映射到特定任务代理，并行执行后合成输出。在韩语复合查询的测试中，多智能体方法在多项指标上显著优于单智能体，尽管延迟和成本有所增加，但整体效果更佳，特别是在个人记录查询方面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 健康检查结果的个性化解读需要结合纵向记录、医学知识、生活方式指导和医疗导航进行推理，这对现有单一智能体构成了挑战。

Method: 构建了一个多智能体LLM系统，能够识别多重意图，将其映射到特定的任务代理，并行执行这些代理的任务，最后综合各代理的输出。研究使用合成的韩语健康检查记录，对比了单智能体和多智能体设置在包含2-4个要求的120条复合查询上的表现。

Result: 多智能体系统的加权LLM-judge评分从1.695提升至1.797（p = 0.027），其他三个LLM法官也显示出一致改进（+0.111至+0.186，均p < 0.05）。提升主要体现在有用性、一致性及对复合查询中所有要求的处理上。两名人类评估者在66.7%和68.3%的成对比较中更偏好多智能体。虽然数值准确性和 grounding 仅在一种情况下显著改善，医疗安全性无差异，关键失败率相当（单智能体15.0% vs 多智能体13.3%）。但多智能体导致延迟增加1.31倍，成本增加2.02倍。改进主要集中在涉及个人记录查询的子群中。

Conclusion: 多智能体LLM系统在复杂的健康检查结果解读任务中，特别是在处理包含多个要求及需要查阅个人记录的复合查询时，能显著提升回答质量和用户偏好度，尽管这以增加计算资源和时间为代价。

Abstract: Personalized interpretation of health checkup results requires reasoning across longitudinal records, medical knowledge, lifestyle guidance, and healthcare navigation. We present a multi-agent large language model (LLM) system that identifies multiple intents, maps each to a task-specific agent, executes them in parallel, and synthesizes their outputs. We compared answers generated in Single Agent and Multi Agent settings on 120 Korean compound queries combining two to four requirements, using synthetic health checkup records. The Multi Agent improved the weighted LLM-judge score from 1.695 to 1.797 (p = 0.027), and three additional LLM judges showed consistent improvements ($Δ$ = +0.111 to +0.186, all p < 0.05). The gains came from usefulness, consistency, and the handling of every requirement in compound queries, whereas numerical accuracy and grounding improved significantly under only one of the four judges and medical safety did not differ, and critical failures occurred at similar rates (Single Agent 15.0% vs. Multi Agent 13.3%). Two human evaluators preferred Multi Agent in 66.7% and 68.3% of pairwise comparisons. Multi Agent execution increased latency and cost by 1.31$\times$ and 2.02$\times$, respectively. In exploratory subgroup analyses, the improvement was concentrated in queries involving personal-record lookup.

</details>
